  const data = JSON.parse(node.textContent);
  const state = {search: '', category: '', warehouse: '', age: '', page: 0};
  const finite = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const esc = value => String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, ch => ({'&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;'}[ch]));
  const qty = value => finite(value) ? new Intl.NumberFormat('zh-CN', {maximumFractionDigits: 2}).format(value) : '待核';
  const range = (lo, hi) => lo === hi ? qty(lo) : qty(lo) + '–' + qty(hi);
  const categories = {incomplete: '缺包待配', excess: '齐套余箱', accessory: '配件 / 耗材'};
  const regionNames = {CA: '美西', GA: '美东', NJ: '美东北'};
  const warehouseLabel = code => (code.startsWith('OWN_') ? '自有仓' : code) + ' · ' + (regionNames[code.split('_').at(-1)] || '区域待核');
  const visible = data.rows.filter(row => row.excessCartons > 0 || row.accessory);
  const bands = data.meta.bands;
  const bySku = new Map();
  for (const row of data.rows) {
    if (!bySku.has(row.sku)) bySku.set(row.sku, []);
    bySku.get(row.sku).push(row);
  }
  let detailOrigin = null;
  function filtered(overrides = {}) {
    const f = {...state, ...overrides};
    const query = f.search.trim().toLowerCase();
    return visible.filter(row => (!query || [row.sku, row.name, row.warehouse, warehouseLabel(row.warehouse), ...row.parts.map(part => part.name)].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(query))
      && (!f.category || row.category === f.category) && (!f.warehouse || row.warehouse === f.warehouse)
      && (!f.age || (f.age === 'unknown' ? !row.ageBounds : (row.ageBounds?.[f.age]?.upper || 0) > 0)));
  }
  function ageTotal(rows, band) {
    const eligible = rows.filter(row => row.ageBounds && finite(row.ageBounds[band]?.lower));
    return {lower: eligible.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.ageBounds[band].lower, 0),
      upper: eligible.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.ageBounds[band].upper, 0), knownGroups: eligible.length,
      unknownGroups: rows.length - eligible.length};
  }
  function metric(label, value, note) {
    return '<div class="ri-metric"><span>' + esc(label) + '</span><strong>' + esc(qty(value)) + '</strong><small>' + esc(note) + '</small></div>';
  }
  function rangeMetric(label, value, note) {
    return '<div class="ri-metric"><span>' + esc(label) + '</span><strong>' + esc(range(value.lower, value.upper)) + '</strong><small>' + esc(note) + '</small></div>';
  }
  function residualUnits(row) { return row.accessory ? row.totalCartons : row.excessCartons; }
  function selectedAgeText(row) {
    if (!state.age) return ageText(row);
    if (state.age === 'unknown') return '库龄待核 ' + qty(residualUnits(row)) + '件；' + ageText(row);
    const value = row.ageBounds?.[state.age];
    return value ? state.age + '：' + range(value.lower, value.upper) + '件' : '当档库龄待核';
  }
  function countsText(row) {
    if (row.accessory) return qty(row.totalCartons) + ' 件 / 包';
    return qty(row.completeSets) + ' 套' + (row.excessCartons ? ' + ' + qty(row.excessCartons) + ' 箱' : '');
  }
  function partsText(row) {
    return Object.entries(row.partQuantities).map(([part, n]) => '包' + part + '/' + row.partCount + '：' + qty(n)).join('；');
  }
  function ageText(row) {
    if (!row.ageBounds) return row.ageStatus === 'quantity_conflict' ? '包件数量不一致，库龄待核' : row.warehouse.startsWith('OWN_') ? '未匹配YQN库龄' : 'YQN库龄待核';
    return bands.filter(band => row.ageBounds[band].upper > 0).map(band => band + ' ' + range(row.ageBounds[band].lower, row.ageBounds[band].upper) + '箱').join('；') || '当前无余箱库龄';
  }
  function skuButton(sku) {
    return '<button type="button" class="ri-sku" data-ri-sku="' + esc(sku) + '">' + esc(sku) + '</button>';
  }
  function groupTable(rows, drawer = false) {
    const perPage = drawer ? rows.length : 60;
    const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(rows.length / perPage));
    if (!drawer) state.page = Math.min(state.page, pageCount - 1);
    const shown = drawer ? rows : rows.slice(state.page * perPage, (state.page + 1) * perPage);
    return '<div class="ri-scroll"><table class="ri-table"><thead><tr><th>库存SKU</th><th>分类</th><th>仓库</th><th>套数 / 余箱</th><th>余箱库龄</th><th>包件</th><th>核对</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
      + shown.map(row => '<tr data-ri-row="' + esc(row.id) + '"><td>' + skuButton(row.sku) + '<small>' + esc(row.name || '名称待补') + '</small></td>'
        + '<td><span class="ri-tag ri-tag-' + esc(row.category) + '">' + esc(row.categoryName) + '</span></td>'
        + '<td>' + esc(warehouseLabel(row.warehouse)) + '<small>' + esc(row.state) + ' · ' + esc(row.version || '原包装') + '</small></td>'
        + '<td><strong>' + esc(countsText(row)) + '</strong><small>' + esc(row.accessory ? '独立物料，不称成品套' : '每套 ' + row.partCount + ' 包；余箱不能凑成完整套') + '</small></td>'
        + '<td><strong>' + esc(drawer ? ageText(row) : selectedAgeText(row)) + '</strong>' + (row.ageBounds ? '<small>区间分别成立，上限不可相加</small>' : '') + '</td>'
        + '<td>' + esc(partsText(row)) + '</td><td>' + (row.ageStatus === 'quantity_conflict' ? '<span class="ri-warning">核包件差异</span>' : row.category === 'incomplete' ? '核缺包 / 预留' : '按实物核对')
        + '<small><button type="button" data-ri-sku="' + esc(row.sku) + '">看库存与包件</button></small></td></tr>').join('')
      + (!shown.length ? '<tr><td colspan="7" class="ri-empty">当前筛选没有记录。</td></tr>' : '') + '</tbody></table></div>'
      + (!drawer && pageCount > 1 ? '<div class="ri-page"><span>' + (state.page + 1) + ' / ' + pageCount + '页</span><button type="button" class="btn" data-ri-page="-1" ' + (!state.page ? 'disabled' : '') + '>上一页</button><button type="button" class="btn" data-ri-page="1" ' + (state.page + 1 >= pageCount ? 'disabled' : '') + '>下一页</button></div>' : '');
  }
  function barChart(items, dimension, selected, unit, note) {
    const max = Math.max(1, ...items.map(item => item.upper ?? item.value ?? 0));
    return '<div class="ri-bars" role="group">' + items.map(item => {
      const upper = item.upper ?? item.value ?? 0;
      const lower = item.lower ?? upper;
      const value = range(lower, upper);
      return '<button type="button" class="ri-bar' + (selected === item.key ? ' is-active' : selected ? ' is-muted' : '') + '" data-ri-dimension="' + dimension + '" data-ri-value="' + esc(item.key) + '" aria-pressed="' + (selected === item.key) + '" title="' + esc(item.label + ' ' + value + unit + '；再点取消') + '">'
        + '<span>' + esc(item.label) + '</span><span class="ri-track"><i style="width:' + 100 * upper / max + '%"></i><b style="width:' + 100 * lower / max + '%"></b></span><strong>' + esc(value) + ' <small>' + esc(unit) + '</small></strong></button>';
    }).join('') + '</div><p class="ri-note">' + esc(note) + '</p>';
  }
  function pendingRows() {
    const query = state.search.trim().toLowerCase();
    const selectedSkus = new Set(filtered().map(row => row.sku));
    return data.pendingYqn.filter(row => (!query || (row.sku + ' ' + row.warehouse).toLowerCase().includes(query))
      && (!state.warehouse || row.warehouse === state.warehouse) && (!state.age && !state.category || selectedSkus.has(row.sku)));
  }
  function pendingTable(rows) {
    return '<details class="ri-card ri-pending" id="riPending"><summary>YQN待核包件 · ' + rows.length + '组</summary><p class="ri-note">只用于差异核对，不加入上方主表库存。缺行保持待核。</p><div class="ri-scroll"><table class="ri-table"><thead><tr><th>SKU</th><th>仓库 / 类型</th><th>主表包件</th><th>YQN包件</th><th>待核事项</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
      + rows.map(row => '<tr><td>' + skuButton(row.sku) + '</td><td>' + esc(warehouseLabel(row.warehouse)) + '<small>' + esc(row.state) + ' · ' + esc(row.version || '原包装') + '</small></td><td>'
        + esc(row.masterPartQuantities ? Object.entries(row.masterPartQuantities).map(([part, n]) => part + '/' + row.partCount + '：' + qty(n)).join('；') : '主表未匹配') + '</td><td>'
        + esc(Object.entries(row.yqnPartQuantities).map(([part, n]) => part + '/' + row.partCount + '：' + qty(n)).join('；')) + '</td><td>' + esc(row.reason) + '</td></tr>').join('')
      + (!rows.length ? '<tr><td colspan="5">当前范围无新增待核包件。</td></tr>' : '') + '</tbody></table></div></details>';
  }
  function render() {
    const host = document.getElementById('piecesPane');
    if (!host) return;
    const rows = filtered();
    const pending = pendingRows();
    const categoryRows = filtered({category: ''});
    const warehouseRows = rows;
    const ageRows = filtered({age: ''});
    const warehouses = [...new Set(visible.map(row => row.warehouse))].sort();
    const categoriesData = Object.entries(categories).map(([key, label]) => ({key, label, value: categoryRows.filter(row => row.category === key).length}));
    const warehousesData = warehouses.map(key => {
      const current = warehouseRows.filter(row => row.warehouse === key);
      const values = state.age && state.age !== 'unknown' ? ageTotal(current, state.age)
        : {value: current.reduce((sum, row) => sum + (state.age === 'unknown' ? residualUnits(row) : row.excessCartons), 0)};
      return {key, label: warehouseLabel(key), ...values};
    }).filter(row => (row.upper ?? row.value) > 0 || state.warehouse === row.key);
    const ages = bands.map(key => ({key, label: key, ...ageTotal(ageRows, key)}));
    const unknown = ageRows.filter(row => !row.ageBounds).length;
    const fullRangeUnits = rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + residualUnits(row), 0);
    const pendingNote = '另列YQN待核余箱 ' + qty(pending.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.yqnExcessCartons, 0)) + '箱 / ' + pending.length + '组，未计主表及当档';
    const ageMetric = state.age && state.age !== 'unknown'
      ? rangeMetric('当档包件 · ' + state.age, ageTotal(rows, state.age), '余箱与配件；命中包装组全龄 ' + qty(fullRangeUnits) + '件，仅对照。' + pendingNote)
      : state.age === 'unknown' ? metric('库龄待核包件', fullRangeUnits, '余箱与配件；数量来自主表。' + pendingNote)
      : metric('主表余箱', rows.filter(row => !row.accessory).reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.excessCartons, 0), '完整套外的包装件。' + pendingNote);
    host.innerHTML = '<div id="riResidualPane"><div class="ri-heading"><div><h2>余箱、缺包与配件</h2><p>库存只显示主表；YQN差异放在待核包件。主表 ' + esc(data.meta.masterDate) + ' · YQN库龄 ' + esc(data.meta.yqnDate) + '。</p></div><button type="button" class="btn" data-ri-export>导出当前清单CSV</button></div>'
      + '<div class="ri-metrics">' + metric('当前SKU', new Set(rows.map(row => row.sku)).size, rows.length + ' 个同仓包装组')
      + ageMetric
      + metric('缺包待配', rows.filter(row => row.category === 'incomplete').length, '当前不能凑齐的包装组')
      + metric('配件 / 耗材', rows.filter(row => row.accessory).reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.totalCartons, 0), '件 / 包，不称成品套') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="ri-card ri-controls"><label>分类<select data-ri-filter="category"><option value="">全部</option>' + Object.entries(categories).map(([key, label]) => '<option value="' + key + '" ' + (state.category === key ? 'selected' : '') + '>' + esc(label) + '</option>').join('') + '</select></label>'
      + '<label>仓库<select data-ri-filter="warehouse"><option value="">全部仓库</option>' + warehouses.map(key => '<option value="' + esc(key) + '" ' + (state.warehouse === key ? 'selected' : '') + '>' + esc(warehouseLabel(key)) + '</option>').join('') + '</select></label>'
      + '<label>搜索<input type="search" data-ri-filter="search" value="' + esc(state.search) + '" placeholder="SKU / 名称 / 仓库" aria-label="搜索残件库存"></label><button type="button" class="btn" data-ri-reset>重置筛选</button><span class="ri-note">' + rows.length + '组 · 图表、数量、明细和导出同步</span></div>'
      + '<div class="ri-charts"><section class="ri-card"><h3>分类 · 包装组</h3>' + barChart(categoriesData, 'category', state.category, '组', '点击分类筛选，其他图表与明细同步；再点取消。') + '</section>'
      + '<section class="ri-card"><h3>仓库 · ' + (state.age ? '当档包件（余箱与配件）' : '主表余箱') + '</h3>' + barChart(warehousesData, 'warehouse', state.warehouse, state.age ? '件' : '箱', state.age ? '与上方当档包件同口径；包含余箱与配件。' : '只统计主表余箱；配件数量在上方单列。') + '</section></div>'
      + '<section class="ri-card ri-age-chart"><div class="ri-heading"><h3>余箱与配件库龄 · YQN证据范围</h3><button type="button" class="btn" data-ri-dimension="age" data-ri-value="unknown" aria-pressed="' + (state.age === 'unknown') + '">库龄待核 ' + unknown + '组</button></div>'
      + barChart(ages, 'age', state.age, '件', '深色为已证下界，浅色为可能上界；每档独立成立，上界不能相加。只在同仓同版本包件数量一致时匹配；档名沿用YQN源表。')
      + (state.age ? '<p class="ri-selected">当前库龄：' + esc(state.age === 'unknown' ? '待核' : state.age) + ' <button type="button" data-ri-clear-age>取消库龄筛选</button></p>' : '') + '</section>'
      + '<section class="ri-card"><div class="ri-heading"><h3>库存明细 · ' + rows.length + '组</h3><span>同仓 / 状态 / 包装版本仅显示一次</span></div>' + groupTable(rows) + '</section>'
      + pendingTable(pending) + '<details class="ri-card"><summary>统计规则与来源</summary><ul>' + data.rules.map(rule => '<li>' + esc(rule) + '</li>').join('') + '</ul>'
      + data.sources.slice(0, 3).map(source => '<p class="ri-source">' + esc(source.path.split('/').at(-1)) + '<br>' + esc(source.mtime) + ' · SHA-256 ' + esc(source.sha256) + '</p>').join('') + '</details></div>';
  }
  function exportRows() {
    const rows = filtered();
    const headers = ['库存SKU', '分类', '仓库', '库存状态', '包装版本', '每套包数', '主表完整套', '主表全龄余箱', '配件件包', '包件数量', '本次库龄筛选', '当档下界件数', '当档上界件数', '库龄说明', '库存来源日期', '库龄来源日期', '说明'];
    const values = rows.map(row => {
      const selected = state.age && state.age !== 'unknown' ? row.ageBounds?.[state.age] : null;
      return [row.sku, row.categoryName, row.warehouse, row.state, row.version || '原包装', row.partCount, row.accessory ? '' : row.completeSets, row.accessory ? '' : row.excessCartons, row.accessory ? row.totalCartons : '', partsText(row), state.age || '全部库龄', selected?.lower ?? '', selected?.upper ?? '', selectedAgeText(row), data.meta.masterDate, data.meta.yqnDate, '库存来自主表；YQN未与主表相加；库龄分段上界不可相加'];
    });
    if (typeof cxDownloadCsv === 'function') return cxDownloadCsv('余箱缺包与配件_' + data.meta.masterDate.replaceAll('-', '') + '.csv', headers, values);
  }
  function detailHtml(sku) {
    const rows = bySku.get(sku) || [];
    if (!rows.length) return '<p class="ri-note">主表未匹配该SKU当前在仓包件；不能按YQN包件直接补入主表库存。</p>';
    const summary = rows[0].masterSummary || {};
    const boxes = ['CA', 'GA', 'NJ'].map(region => '<div class="ri-metric"><span>' + regionNames[region] + ' · 主表</span><strong>' + qty(summary[region]?.available) + ' 套</strong><small>可用；预留 ' + qty(summary[region]?.reserved) + ' · 在途 ' + qty(summary[region]?.transit) + '</small></div>').join('');
    return '<section class="ri-detail"><h3>当前库存</h3><div class="ri-metrics">' + boxes + '</div><p class="ri-note">主表 ' + esc(data.meta.masterDate) + '。三区各显示一次，缺行保持待核。下方包件为物理配套，不与上方可用套相加。</p>'
      + '<h3>仓库、库龄与包件</h3>' + groupTable(rows, true)
      + rows.map(row => '<details class="ri-card"><summary>' + esc(warehouseLabel(row.warehouse)) + ' · ' + esc(row.version || '原包装') + ' · 原始包件</summary><div class="ri-scroll"><table class="ri-table"><thead><tr><th>包件码</th><th>数量</th><th>柜号</th><th>尺寸 / 体积</th><th>内部日期（对账）</th><th>源行</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
        + row.parts.map(part => '<tr><td>' + esc(part.barcode) + '</td><td>' + qty(part.quantity) + '箱</td><td>' + esc(part.container || '待核') + '</td><td>' + esc(part.dimensionsCm.map(qty).join('×')) + 'cm<small>' + (finite(part.cbm) ? part.cbm.toFixed(6) + 'm³' : '体积待核') + '</small></td><td>' + esc(String(part.internalInbound || '待核').slice(0, 10)) + '<small>内部库龄 ' + qty(part.internalAgeDays) + '天，仅对账</small></td><td>' + esc(part.source.sheet + '!' + part.source.row) + '</td></tr>').join('') + '</tbody></table></div></details>').join('') + '</section>';
  }
  renderPieces = render;
  if (typeof currentPieceGroups === 'function') currentPieceGroups = () => filtered();
  if (typeof exportPiecesCsv === 'function') exportPiecesCsv = exportRows;
  const oldOpen = typeof cxOpenSku === 'function' ? cxOpenSku : null;
  if (oldOpen) cxOpenSku = function (sku) {
    if (!(typeof cxState !== 'undefined' && cxState.view === 'pieces')) detailOrigin = null;
    const result = oldOpen.apply(this, arguments);
    if (detailOrigin === sku) {
      cxState.detail.tab = 'stock';
      cxRenderSku();
    }
    return result;
  };
  const oldDetail = typeof cxRenderSku === 'function' ? cxRenderSku : null;
  if (oldDetail) cxRenderSku = function () {
    const result = oldDetail.apply(this, arguments);
    if (detailOrigin === cxState.detail?.sku) {
      const tabs = document.getElementById('cxDetailTabs');
      const stock = tabs?.querySelector('[data-cx-detail-tab="stock"]');
      if (stock) { stock.textContent = '库存 / 库龄 / 包件'; tabs.prepend(stock); }
      const subtitle = document.getElementById('cxDetailSubtitle');
      if (subtitle) subtitle.textContent = '先看库存、库龄与包件；销售和利润可切换查看';
      if (cxState.detail.tab === 'stock') {
        const body = document.getElementById('cxDetailBody');
        const details = document.createElement('details'); details.className = 'cx-fold';
        const summary = document.createElement('summary'); summary.textContent = '其他库存、在途与历史对账资料';
        const legacy = document.createElement('div'); legacy.className = 'cx-fold-body';
        while (body.firstChild) legacy.appendChild(body.firstChild);
        details.append(summary, legacy);
        const primary = document.createElement('div'); primary.innerHTML = detailHtml(cxState.detail.sku);
        body.append(primary, details);
      }
    }
    return result;
  };
  document.addEventListener('click', event => {
    const target = event.target.closest('[data-ri-dimension],[data-ri-reset],[data-ri-export],[data-ri-clear-age],[data-ri-page],[data-ri-sku]');
    if (!target) return;
    event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation();
    if (target.hasAttribute('data-ri-sku')) { detailOrigin = target.dataset.riSku; if (typeof cxOpenSku === 'function') cxOpenSku(detailOrigin); return; }
    if (target.hasAttribute('data-ri-export')) { exportRows(); return; }
    if (target.hasAttribute('data-ri-page')) { state.page = Math.max(0, state.page + Number(target.dataset.riPage)); render(); return; }
    if (target.hasAttribute('data-ri-reset')) Object.assign(state, {search: '', category: '', warehouse: '', age: '', page: 0});
    else if (target.hasAttribute('data-ri-clear-age')) { state.age = ''; state.page = 0; }
    else if (target.dataset.riDimension) { const key = target.dataset.riDimension; state[key] = state[key] === target.dataset.riValue ? '' : target.dataset.riValue; state.page = 0; }
    render();
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('change', event => {
    const key = event.target.dataset.riFilter;
    if (!key || key === 'search') return;
    state[key] = event.target.value; state.page = 0; render();
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('input', event => {
    if (event.target.dataset.riFilter !== 'search') return;
    const position = event.target.selectionStart;
    state.search = event.target.value; state.page = 0; render();
    const search = document.querySelector('#riResidualPane [data-ri-filter="search"]');
    search?.focus(); try { search?.setSelectionRange(position, position); } catch (_) { /* search输入不支持选择区时保留焦点。 */ }
  }, true);
  window.residualInventoryModel = {ready: true, data, state, filtered, ageTotal, pendingRows, render, detailHtml, countsText, exportRows};
  const marker = document.createElement('span'); marker.hidden = true; marker.id = 'residualInventoryReady'; document.body.appendChild(marker);
  if (typeof pageState !== 'undefined' && pageState.view === 'pieces') render();
})();

</script>

<style data-dashboard-update="reason_chart.css">
#raPane .ra-store-breakdown {
  border-top: 1px solid #e7edf3;
  margin-top: 12px;
  padding-top: 10px;
}
#raPane .ra-store-breakdown h4 {
  font-size: 14px;
  margin: 0 0 8px;
}
#raPane .ra-reason-legend {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 6px 16px;
  color: #41566b;
  font-size: 12px;
  margin: 10px 0 8px;
}
#raPane .ra-reason-legend span {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 5px;
}
#raPane .ra-reason-legend span::before {
  background: var(--reason-color);
  border-radius: 2px;
  content: '';
  width: 12px;
  height: 8px;
}
#raPane .ra-reason-original { --reason-color: #496f9e; }

<script data-dashboard-update="detail_subtitle.js">
(function () {
  'use strict';
  const { masterDate, yqnDate } = window.residualInventoryModel.data.meta;
  const subtitle = document.getElementById('subtitle');

  function updateSubtitle(view) {
    if (view === 'pieces' && subtitle) {
      subtitle.textContent = '库存主表 ' + masterDate + ' · YQN库龄证据 ' + yqnDate + ' · 按仓库和库龄查看余箱、缺包与配件';
    }
  }

  const openView = setPageView;
  setPageView = function (view) {
    const result = openView.apply(this, arguments);
    updateSubtitle(view);
    return result;
  };
  updateSubtitle(pageState.view);
})();

</script>
